UMUTWE WA              UMUCO NYARWANDA                                                                     AMASAHA         48
MBERE
                       Gusoma no gusesengura imyandiko ijyanye n'umuco nyarwanda;                                          Ikito
Ubushobozi bw'ingenzi  Gukoresha neza mu mvugo no mu nyandiko ikeshamvugo;                                                 nder
bugamijwe:             Gukoresha neza mu mvugo no mu nyandiko ibinyazina;                                                  wa
                       Guhanga indirimbo no kuyiririmba;
                       Gutandukanya amoko y'insigamigani.
